Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The holomorphic operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 351. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 32 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by holomorphic generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 351, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 32. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the holomorphic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 2. The moduli space has dimension at most 108 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 2 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the holomorphic structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 108. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
